企業簡介
Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group Limited (the “Group”;              周 大 福 珠 寶 集 團 有 限 公 司 (「 集 團 」； 香 港 聯 交 所 股 份
SEHK stock code: 1929) listed on the Main Board of               代號： 1929 )於 2011 年 12 月在香港聯合交易所主板上市，
The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong in December 2011.                以成為全球最值得信賴的珠寶集團為願景。
The Group’s vision is to become the most trusted
jewellery group in the world.

Founded in 1929, the Group’s iconic brand                        集 團 標 誌 性 品 牌「 周 大 福 」創 立 於 1929 年 ， 被 廣 獲 認 同
“CHOW TAI FOOK” is widely recognised for its                     為信譽及正貨的象徵，並以產品設計、品質與價值聞名。
trustworthiness and authenticity, and is renowned for its        集團及其標誌性品牌的成功，從過去 90 年的悠久歷史，
product design, quality and value. A long-standing               體現出對創新和工藝的持久堅持，以及恒久抱持的核心
commitment to innovation and craftsmanship has                   價值「真誠t永恒」。
contributed to the Group’s success, along with that of its
iconic retail brand, and has been embodied in its rich
90 year heritage. Underpinning this success are our long

    Chairman’s Statement
    主席報告書




                                                        This year marks a significant moment for the
                                                        Group, a time in history which we are proud
                                                        to have reached. Drawing on our passion for
                                                        “Sincerity • Eternity”, Chow Tai Fook shaped
                                                        the course of history with amazing
                                                        innovations along the way.

                                                        今年是本集團的重要時刻和里程碑。周大福
                                                        本著對「真誠•永恒」的熱忱與堅持，時刻
                                                        以無比創意，牽頭為行業締造歷史。

                                                        Dr. Cheng Kar-Shun, Henry 鄭家純博士
                                                        Chairman 主席




    Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group Limited 周大福珠寶集團有限公司
                                                                                                 Chairman’s Statement    9
                                                                                                            主席報告書




Dear Shareholders,                                                     各位股東：
2019 marks our 90th Anniversary. I feel immensely grateful that        2019 年是周大福創立 90 周年誌慶。周大福跨越 90 載
Chow Tai Fook has straddled nine decades with a tremendous             取得非凡成就，奠定深厚根基，本人對此銘感至深。
success and strong heritage. Adhering to our core values               周 大 福 珠 寶 集 團 一 直 秉 承「真 誠 • 永 恒」核 心 價 值，
of “Sincerity • Eternity” and staying focused in our core strengths,   專注發揮核心本業優勢，因此得以蓬勃發展，成為舉
Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group has thrived and become a world-          世知名的珠寶企業。
renowned jewellery enterprise.


Performance Review                                                     表現回顧
I am pleased to present FY2019 annual results of the Group.            本 人 欣 然 報 告 本 集 團 2019 財 政 年 度 的 全 年 業 績。
FY2019 was a year of inflexion. The Group enjoyed a smooth             2019 財政年度可謂迂迴曲折：上半年度各項業務進展
sailing in 1HFY2019, while it was overcast with macro uncertainties    平順，惟下半年度由於宏觀環境的變化，為本集團增
in 2HFY2019. Yet, the Group’s underlying competence remained           添不明朗因素。然而，本集團根基鞏固、實力雄厚，
strong and the Group managed to drive growth in FY2019 against         足 以 抗 衡 經 濟 逆 境， 於 2019 財 政 年 度 仍 能 錄 得 業 績
the backdrop of the headwinds. Our “Smart+ 2020” three-year            增長。這亦有賴我們於 2018 財政年度強勢展開及貫徹
strategic plan had a strong start since FY2018 also helped             執行「Smart+ 2020」三年策略框架，為 2019 財政年
contributing to the revival of the operations in FY2019.               度的業務表現重拾動力。


During FY2019, the Group’s revenue rose by 12.7% year-on-year          本 集 團 2019 財 政 年 度 的 營 業 額 按 年 增 長 12.7% 至
to HK$66,661 million. Core operating profit surged 24.4%. Profit       66,661 百萬港元。主要經營溢利飆升 24.4%。本公司
attributable to shareholders of the Company grew by 11.8% to           股東應佔溢利上升 11.8% 至 4,577 百萬港元，每股盈
HK$4,577 million, while earnings per share was HK$0.46.                利為 0.46 港元。


The Board has proposed a final dividend and a special dividend of      董 事 會 建 議 派 發 末 期 股 息 每 股 0.20 港 元 及 特 別 股 息
HK$0.20 and HK$0.30 per share, respectively. The payout ratio,         每股 0.30 港元。撇除特別股息，2019 財政年度派息率
excluding special dividend, approximates 76.5% in FY2019.              約相等於 76.5%。

history which we are proud to have reached. Drawing on our                 對「真 誠 • 永 恒」的 熱 忱 與 堅 持，時 刻 以 無 比 創 意，

course of history with amazing innovations along the way.                  藉此實現業務持續發展，並且屢創新猷，引領珠寶行
Committed to nurturing high-calibre staff, the Group in turn               業向前邁進。最近 T MARK 的區塊鏈技術應用項目，
achieves continuous development and sets benchmarks to shape               正 是 破 格 創 新 出 色 的 典 範。 作 為 高 瞻 遠 矚 的 珠 寶 業
the jewellery industry. Our latest blockchain initiative on T MARK is      巨 擘， 本 人 認 為 變 革 轉 型 是 一 個 永 無 休 止 的 歷 程，

standard. As a visionary jewellery titan, I think the transformation is    致 力 為 顧 客 帶 來 完 美 無 縫、 獨 特 不 凡 的 體 驗。 本 人
an endless journey that edges us to keep pace with times and stay          深信，只要我們齊心努力、堅持創新，今後必將繼續

unique customer experience. I am confident that if we work as

     Annual Results at a Glance
     年度業績概覽

     Managing Director’s Strategic Report
     董事總經理策略報告



                                                               FY2019 marked another year of growth for our
                                                               business. Our core competence as an innovative and
                                                               agile industry leader has yielded positive results.
                                                               我們的業務於 2019 財政年度繼續錄得增長。作為創新
                                                               靈活的行業領導者，在我們自身優勢及核心競爭力下，
                                                               取得不俗的成果。
                                                               Mr. Wong Siu-Kee, Kent 黃紹基先生
                                                               Managing Director 董事總經理




     FY2019 marked another year of growth for our business. Despite a         我 們 的 業 務 於 2019 財 政 年 度 繼 續 錄 得 增 長。 儘 管
     dampened macro-economic outlook due to continued US-China                2019 財政年度下半年中美貿易緊張局勢持續，動搖了
     trade tensions in 2HFY2019, our core competence as an innovative         宏觀經濟前景，但作為創新靈活的行業領導者，在我
     and agile industry leader has yielded positive results.                  們自身優勢及核心競爭力下，取得不俗的成果。


     We have three long-term goals, namely market share expansion,            我們有三大長遠目標，分別為擴大市場佔有率、提升
     customer experience improvement and stable returns to our                顧 客 體 驗， 以 及 為 股 東 提 供 穩 健 回 報。 為 達 成 上 述
     shareholders. We have strategies and plans in place across the           目標，我們制定了相關策略及計劃，於整個集團貫徹
     Group to ensure that we achieve these objectives.                        實行。


     Our “Smart+ 2020” strategic framework, a three-year strategic            我們於 2018 財政年度推行的「Smart+ 2020」策略框
     plan focused on enhancing customer experience, launched in               架，為我們提供強大的增長動力，「Smart+ 2020」策
     FY2018, has provided us with powerful impetus for growth. Over           略框架是一個為期三年的策略性工作計劃，專注於提
     the last year, we have achieved several exciting strategic               升顧客體驗。自策略框架執行後，我們成功締造多項
     milestones as a result. Through the relentless and diligent              令 人 雀 躍 的 新 里 程。 通 過 堅 持 不 懈 地 執 行
     execution of our “Smart+ 2020” strategic framework, we are              「Smart+ 2020」策略框架，我們定能為顧客提供無縫
     confident that we can deliver a seamless and compelling customer         且非凡的購物體驗，為股東創造長遠價值。
     experience that ensures we create long-term shareholder value.

                                                                                            Managing Director’s Strategic Report   21
                                                                                                              董事總經理策略報告




Business Development                                                        業務發展
Customers’ preference in our key markets have changed rapidly               過去五年，我們主要市場的顧客喜好變化相當迅速，
over the last five years. Some themes are common and some are               部份原因是環境大趨勢，而部份個別因素則於相關特
specific to their respective markets. Nowadays, consumers are               定 市 場 才 出 現。 迄 今， 隨 著 消 費 者 品 味 越 見 提 升，
increasingly sophisticated to seek more personalised products and           他 們 對 個 性 化 產 品 和 專 屬 體 驗 的 需 求 均 有 所 提 高，
exclusive experiences. This phenomenon is particularly true for             此情況於相對富裕的顧客群而言更為明顯。至於年輕
relatively affluent groups. Younger customers, on the other hand,           顧客群，他們選擇品牌時會以手機、社交媒體及網上
focus on mobile, social media and online experiences as an                  的體驗為重要基準。基本上，上述兩種情況均可見於
important benchmarks to evaluate their selection of brands. These           我們所有主要市場。
two common themes are generally applicable across all of our key markets.


Mainland China shares these common themes, and has the                      中 國 內 地 除 出 現 上 述 普 遍 情 況 外， 由 於 地 域 廣 泛 及
additional challenge of being a vast and geographically diverse             市場多元化，使到零售渠道較為分散，為該市場帶來
market with dispersed retail channels that are forcing brand owners         額外的挑戰，迫使品牌及零售商需不時檢視其網絡和
and retailers to reposition their network and channel strategies to         渠道策略，做到與時並進，才可緊貼市場的步伐。
stay relevant.


In Hong Kong and Macau, retailers have to deal with longer-term             於香港及澳門，零售商必須應對較深遠的挑戰，如在
challenges, such as perusing incremental growth in these relatively         這兩個相對成熟的市場爭取持續的增長，以及同時應
mature markets, along with the evolving travel and spending                 對入境旅客旅遊和消費喜好的轉變。
preferences of inbound visitors.


In overseas markets, particularly in Southeast Asia, we see                 至於海外市場，尤其東南亞地區，我們看到正待全面
potential opportunities that we have yet to fully explore. Southeast        開 發 的 潛 在 商 機。 東 南 亞 國 家 從 全 球 化 趨 勢 中 獲 益
Asian countries have greatly benefitted from globalisation trends.          匪淺。過去數年，該等國家的產能迅速增加，以應對
Their production capacity has increased rapidly in response to rising       中國內地不斷上漲的勞工成本。鑒於有愈來愈多且富
labour costs in Mainland China over the past few years. With the            裕的中產階級在這些亞洲經濟體系中冒起，我們相信
emergence of a larger and richer middle class in those Asian                當地對優質產品及服務的需求將會相當殷切。
economies, we believe that there will be huge demand for quality
products and services.


As for the scale of the global diamond jewellery market, it stood at        環 顧 2017 年 全 球 鑽 石 珠 寶 市 場 的 市 值 達 到 820 億
sheering size of US$82 billion in 2017(1). The United States alone          美 元 (1)， 美 國 已 佔 了 全 球 50% 的 市 場 佔 有 率。 縱 觀
has captured 50% of the global market share. Looking across the             美國鑽石市場的價值鏈，主要圍繞功能性且高度分散，
United States diamond market’s value chain, which is highly                 因此我們相信仍有充裕的空間供我們通過固有的能力
fragmented and organised around functions, we believe that there            為上、中、下游增值。
is ample room for us to add value to the upstream, midstream and
downstream through our built-in capabilities.

     Information technology and data governance                               資訊科技及數據管治

     IT governance framework                                                  資訊科技管治框架
     • We formalised our IT Governance Framework, that has been in            • 我 們 自 2017 財 政 年 度 正 式 實 行 資 訊 科 技 管 治
        place since FY2017, covering four key elements:                         框架，其中涵蓋四大元素：

        º   Business alignment – to provide a structure for aligning IT         º   配合業務發展 — 提供與商業策略和優次相符的
            strategies and priorities with business strategies and                  資訊科技策略及架構，合理地分配集團內部重要
            priorities. Provide a framework that guides the allocation of           的資訊科技決策。
            important IT decisions to different parties within the Group.

        º   Resources management – to prioritise and allocate resources         º   資源管理 — 將資源按優次等級分配至不同的
            across different IT projects.                                           資訊科技項目。

        º   Performance measurement – to set up a mechanism and a               º   績效計量 — 建立評估項目成果績效的機制和
            platform for evaluating the performance of project outcome.             平台。

        º   Risk management – to monitor and manage IT related risks.           º   風險管理 — 監察和管理與資訊科技相關的風險。

     • Our main objective is to build differentiating IT capabilities, that   • 我們的主要目標為建立具差異化的資訊科技實力，
        fully support the execution of our business strategies.                 使之能全力支持我們業務策略的執行。

     • During FY2019, we have enhanced our demand and project                 • 於 2019 財 政 年 度， 我 們 加 強 了 集 團 的 需 求 和
        management mechanism, to ensure our IT resources are being              項目管理機制，以確保資訊科技的資源以有效方式
        prioritised in effective ways that maximise value creation for          作優次排序，從而為集團創造最大價值。
        the Group.

     • As data is a vital asset for our business development, we              • 由 於 數 據 為 集 團 業 務 發 展 的 重 要 資 產， 故 此 我 們

        enhancing operational efficiencies. This also paves the way for         數碼化轉型作好準備。
        our digital transformation.

     • Our programme focuses on three key areas that ensure data              • 此計劃針對如可確保數據質素達到最高水平的三大
        quality is up to the highest standard:                                  關鍵領域：

            effectiveness for setting up data standards and quality at              生 命 週 期 的 每 個 階 段 建 立 數 據 的 標 準 及 質 量，

            archive of data.

            and secure the data properly.                                           且將之保密。

government such as lowering reserve requirement ratio, various           降低存款儲備金率、推出各項減稅措施，增加尤其是

     財務狀況


     Despite macro uncertainties in 2HFY2019, our underlying competencies
     remained strong and our profitability demonstrated continuous
     improvement over the past three financial years.

     在過往三個財政年度的盈利持續錄得增長。

     • Unrealised loss (gain) for the year represents the net effect of (i)   • 年內未變現虧損（收益）指 (i) 撥回於上一個財政年度
       the reversal of the loss (gain) recorded due to the timing               完結時因確認黃金好倉與淡倉影響之時間差所產生
       difference in recognising the effect of long and short position in       的虧損（收益）與 (ii) 於當前財政年度結算時，因相關
       gold when we take a snapshot position at the end of the                  的時間差而產生的虧損（收益）的淨影響。
       previous financial year; and (ii) the loss (gain) arising from such
       timing difference at the end of the current financial year.
     • We use gold loans (short position in gold) for economic hedge          • 我們利用黃金借貸（黃金淡倉）為經濟避險目的以減
       purpose to mitigate the financial impact of the gold price              輕黃金存貨（黃金好倉）價格波動的財務影響。雖然
       fluctuations in our gold inventories (long position). While the         黃金好淡倉的長線影響預期將會在出售黃金產品時
       long-term effect of long and short positions in gold is expected        互相抵銷，惟對報告期末的持倉狀況而言，計入銷
       to net out each other through the sales of gold products, a loss        售成本的黃金借貸虧損（收益）與已對沖黃金存貨的
       (gain) may arise due to a short-term timing difference between          銷售之間或會有短暫的時間差，從而可能產生虧損
       the time when a loss (gain) on gold loans is recorded in the cost       （收益）。
       of goods sold and the time when sales of hedged gold
       inventories are recognised, when we take a snapshot position
       at the end of the reporting period.

• The Group’s daily operation was mainly financed by operating          • 本集團日常業務所需資金主要來自經營現金流量，
  cash flows, and mainly relied on short-term borrowings to               亦會主要以短期借貸滿足旺季時的存貨資金需求、
  satisfy inventory financing needs during peak seasons, working          未 來 擴 展 計 劃 所 需 營 運 資 金 及 預 計 之 外 的 需 求。
  capital for future expansion plans and unexpected needs. The            本集團於償還貸款方面並無任何困難。
  Group has not experienced any difficulties in repaying its
  borrowings.

• The Group’s income and expenditure were mostly denominated            • 本 集 團 之 收 入 及 開 支 主 要 以 港 元 及 人 民 幣 計 值，
  in HKD and RMB, while its assets and liabilities were mostly            而資產及負債則主要以港元、人民幣及美元計值。
  denominated in HKD, RMB and USD.


Effect of RMB fluctuation                                               人民幣波動的影響
• As part of our business operation was in Mainland China, the          • 由於我們有部分業務在中國內地經營，人民幣的波
  fluctuation in RMB would post some impact to our                        動對我們的表現構成若干影響。
  performance.

• Transactions entered by the Hong Kong entities but                    • 香港公司以人民幣計值的交易（包括與中國內地附
  denominated in RMB, including the inter-group transactions              屬公司進行的集團間交易）會按交易當天的現貨匯
  with the Mainland China subsidiaries, are converted into HKD,           率兌換為港元，即本集團的功能貨幣，而未結算的
  the functional currency of the Group, initially using the spot rate     交易則會以收市匯率於結算日換算。現貨匯率與收
  at the date of transaction and the unsettled transactions are           市匯率的匯兌收益或虧損於損益賬中確認，而人民
  retranslated at closing exchange rate at the balance sheet date.        幣貶值會對我們的年內溢利構成負面影響。
  Such translation differences between the spot rate and closing
  exchange rate are recognised in profit or loss, negatively
  affecting our profit for the year when RMB depreciated.

• Exchange difference also arises when i) incomes and expenses          • 此外，匯兌差異源於 i）中國內地分部的收支按平均
  of the Mainland China segment are translated into HKD, the              匯率換算為港元，即本集團呈列貨幣，而相應的資
  presentation currency of the Group, at the average exchange             產及負債按收市匯率換算；及 ii）中國內地分部的資
  rate, while the corresponding assets and liabilities are translated     產淨值於本財政年度的收市匯率較上個財政年度的
  at closing exchange rate and ii) change in closing exchange             收市匯率有所變動。有關差異於權益中的換算儲備
  rates at the current financial year of the net assets of the            內確認入賬。
  Mainland China segment from the closing rates at the previous
  financial year. Such differences are recognised in the translation
  reserve in equity.

     • Revenue and core operating profit on constant exchange rate           • 按相同匯率計算的營業額及主要經營溢利乃使用中
        basis are calculated by translating current year’s revenue and            國內地分部於本年度以人民幣計值的營業額及主要

Stakeholder Approach                                                       以持份者為本的方針
• The Board has adopted a stakeholder approach to foster                   • 董事會已採納以持份者為本的方針，本著透明、互
   relations with a wider range of stakeholders both inside and             動與公平對待的原則，與本集團內外更廣泛的持份
   outside the Group based on the principles of transparency,               者建立關係。
   interactive engagement and fair treatment.


• In the pursuit of good corporate governance, our relationship with       • 為實行良好企業管治，我們與持份者的關係對本集
   stakeholders is critical to the long-term performance of the Group       團長遠表現和我們股東的價值相當重要。此乃由於
   and our shareholders’ value. This is because by maintaining a            與持份者維持緊密關係，將有助董事會作出更完善
   close connection with stakeholders, it fosters the growth of our         的決策，建構有效支持業務營運的生態系統，促進
   value creation chain by enabling better Board decisions and              我們價值鏈的發展。
   creating a supportive ecosystem for the operations.


Enabling better board decisions                                            協助董事會作出更完善決策
• From the Board’s point of view, engaging with a wider range of           • 從董事會的角度，使更廣泛的持份者參與有助識別
   stakeholders helps to identify emerging trends of social needs           社會需要和商業機遇的新興趨勢；以不同持份者的
   and business opportunities; enhance our capacity to innovate by          洞見，提升我們的創新能力；制定優質策略和「以
   feeding in stakeholders’ lines of sight; foster high quality strategy    客為本」的業務模式；識別出本集團與持份者達致
   and customer-centric business model; identify synergies between          的協同效益，有助促成合作和綜合解決方案，應對
   the Group and our stakeholders which encourage collaborations            未來的挑戰。
   and integrated solutions to future challenges.


• Examples of our approaches to the key stakeholders in                    • 與上述有關以主要持份者為本的方針的例子包括主
   connection with the above include proactive investor relations,          動建立投資者關係、與業務夥伴進行雙贏合作、執
   win-win collaboration with business partners, customer-driven            行 顧 客 主 導 的「 Smart+ 2020」策 略 框 架、為 承 傳
   “Smart+ 2020” strategic framework, partnership with non-profit           工藝和培育創新與非牟利組織合作發展社區項目、
   organisations on community projects for preserving                       以及多元化董事會以連繫更廣泛的持份者。
   craftsmanship and nurturing innovation, and a diversified Board
   which engages with a broad spectrum of stakeholders.


Creating a supportive ecosystem for the operations                         建構有效支持業務營運的生態系統
• In view of facilitating corporate management, engaging with              • 提升企業管理方面，持份者的參與有助連接業務與
   stakeholders assists the business to gain access to a                    支持其營運的生態系統，其中可提供寶貴的人力資
   supportive ecosystem, where valuable human capital and                   本和社會資源，同時減低業務經營的屏障和阻礙。
   social resources are available and barriers and resistance to
   the business are lessened.


• We embed into our operations the people-oriented culture and             • 我們將以人為本的文化和為更廣泛持份者創造價值
   value creation objectives for a wider range of stakeholders              的目標灌輸入我們的業務經營，拉近人與人之間距
   which gather our people working together for meaningful                  離，合力建立更美好的社區。
   purposes beneficial to the communities at large.

      • While mutual trust is paramount to the success of our business,      • 互信對我們業務的成功極為重要，故此我們通過主

         the Company. We also hold press and analyst conferences at            分析員招待會，該等會議每年最少舉行兩次。
         least twice a year after releasing the interim and annual results.

         quarter end.

         disclosures with the aim of keeping the market regularly

  by shareholders on the performance of the Group. Shareholders       以留步繼續跟董事總經理和執行董事（財務及營運）

  Director and Executive Director, Finance and Operations for

  ratio, free cash flows and so on.

  therefore always keen to engage with investors.                     也踴躍跟投資者交流。

  inside information policy.

  media and analyst reports, document feedback from investor          報告，記錄來自投資者會議的反饋以了解市場的期
  meetings in order to understand market expectations.                望。

  particular piece of information on the Group’s circumstances is

      Fair treatment of shareholders                                        公平對待所有股東
      • Shareholder analysis, conducted every quarter, enables us to        • 每季進行的股東構成分析，讓我們檢討投資者關係
         review our investor relations strategy and work plan. Through       的策略和工作計劃。我們希望藉著適當安排路演和
         well-planned roadshows and investor conferences, we                 投 資 者 會 議， 可 以 向 投 資 市 場 展 示 公 司 的 長 期 價
         endeavour to make long-term value of the Company visible to         值，從而提升所有股東的投資回報。
         the investment community at large, thereby enhancing the
         investment return to all shareholders.


      • No selective disclosure of inside information is allowed in our     • 本公司概不允許選擇性披露內幕消息。我們已設立
         Company. We have formalised controls on the sources of              制度在資料來源和溝通渠道方面加以規範。
         information and the communication channels.


      • We emphasise the integrity and consistency in the content of        • 我們用心編製公司年報和中期報告，務求保持內容
         the annual and interim reports, which contain information           中肯及一致，而且投資者和公眾都可以在其中獲得
         desired by investors and the public.                                需要的資訊。


      • This set of published information is used in investor meetings      • 我們在投資者會議和研討會中，亦沿用這些已公開
         and conferences.                                                    的資料。


      • According to our spokesperson policy, only designated               • 設發言人制度，指定執行董事及投資者關係及企業
         executive directors and the investor relations and corporate        傳訊團隊成員方可代表公司向投資者或媒體發言或
         communications team are authorised to speak to or hold              召開會議。我們備存會議筆錄和通話記錄以備日後
         meetings with investors or media on behalf of the                   參考。
         Company. Attendance notes and call logs are properly kept
         for future reference.

         out a quiet period to refrain from investor relations activities    定講稿及問答內容以管理對外溝通的訊息；及業績

      Dividend information                                                    股息資料
      • It is the Company’s policy to strike a balance between making         • 本公司的股息政策在於善用資本以鞏固業務發展以
          an efficient use of capital to strengthen our business                及與股東分享盈利成果之間取得平衡。本公司並無
          development and rewarding our shareho l d e r s w i t h a             預設的派息率。在決定派付股息金額或形式時，董
          participation in the Company’s profits. The Company does not          事會會考慮本集團的現金流量及財務狀況、其盈利
          have a pre-determined dividend payout ratio. In determining           能力以及外圍經濟狀況、其業務發展計劃和資本需
          the amount and form of dividend, the Board will consider the          求、 本 公 司 向 其 附 屬 公 司 可 收 取 的 股 息 及 稅 務 考
          Group’s cash flows and financial position, its earnings capacity      量、派息有否限制、其他適用法律法規以及董事會
          and the external economic conditions, its business                    認為合適的任何其他因素。在沒有特殊及預計以外
          development plans and capital requirements, the dividend              的情況下，本公司每年擬宣派兩次股息。儘管上文
          receivable by the Company from its subsidiaries and taxation          所述，我們無法保證於任何財政期間分派股息的金
          considerations, any restrictions on the payment of dividends,         額或比率。
          other applicable laws and regulations, and any other factors
          that the Board considers appropriate. In the absence of special
          or unexpected circumstances, the Company intends to declare
          dividends twice a year. Notwithstanding all the above, we
          cannot assure any amount or ratio for the distribution of
          dividends in any financial period.

      Board Governance Framework and Major Tasks                               董事會管治框架與主要工作
      • The Board’s role is to provide leadership for the Company and          • 董事會的角色為領導公司，並作為獨立決策機構為
         direction for the management as an independent decision-               管理層訂立方向。董事會就本集團的表現集體負責
         making body. Our Board is collectively responsible and                 及對股東問責。
         accountable to our shareholders for the Group’s performance.


      • The Nomination Committee, Remuneration Committee and                   • 提名委員會、薪酬委員會和審核委員會分別承擔董
         Audit Committee each has its respective terms of reference             事會指定的職責，進行具體的管治工作。這些委員
         specified by the Board to undertake detailed governance work.          會 2019 財 政 年 度 的 工 作 於 前 頁 圖 表 簡 述，詳 情 見
         A brief summary of their tasks during FY2019 is shown on the           以下章節。
         chart in the preceding page and further mentioned in the
         sections below.


      • The Executive Committee is delegated with all the powers,              • 執行委員會獲授所有權力、權限和酌情權，根據其
         authorities and discretion to act for the Board according to its       於本集團一般業務過程有關的所有營運、管理和行
         terms of reference in all operational, managerial and                  政職能的職權範圍為董事會行事，負責監察本集團
         administrative functions concerning the Group’s ordinary course        策略規劃的執行情況和集團所有業務單位的營運。
         of business. It monitors the execution of the Group’s strategic
         plans and the operations of all business units of the Group.


      • Matters beyond its delegated authority are reserved to the             • 超越執行委員會職權範圍的事宜須留待董事會決
         Board for decision, including those transactions which are             定，包括根據上市規則須予披露的交易和非豁免關
         notifiable transactions or non-exempt connected transactions           連交易；執行董事單獨或與他人共同於其中有重大
         under the Listing Rules; or in which an executive director has         權益的交易；和非常重大或不尋常事宜。
         (individually or together with others) material interest; and those
         matters of onerous or unusual nature.


      • The roles of our Chairman and Managing Director are clearly            • 主席和董事總經理的角色有清晰界定，董事會和管
         defined and there is an effective segregation of duties between        理層的職責亦有明確區分。主席鄭家純博士領導董
         the Board and the management. Our Chairman, Dr. Cheng Kar-             事會，及確保董事會有效運作和適時討論所有重要
         Shun, Henry, provides leadership for the Board and ensures that        事項。董事總經理黃紹基先生領導本集團營運和業
         the Board works effectively and all important issues are               務發展。
         discussed in a timely manner. Our Managing Director, Mr.
         Wong Siu-Kee, Kent, takes the lead in managing the Group’s

      • In respect of our approach to connected transactions, we work       • 在關連交易方面，我們的方針是積極開發關連公司
        together with related companies mainly for collaborating efforts     之間在市場拓展方面的合作商機，各自發揮所長，
        in marketing to explore value-added services for our common          目的為重叠的顧客群引入更多增值服務，增強我們

      • When approving a connected transaction, apart from the              • 獨立董事審議關連交易除了協議的條款的公平性之

        the proposal to the business of the Group.

      • It is a norm for the director who is a connected person in          • 建立董事會的常規，即使需予批准的關連交易，與相
        relation to the connected transaction tabled for approval to         關連的董事並不屬於重大利益，這些董事也會於審議
        declare his interest in advance and then abstain from voting on      前申報利益關係，並不參與批准有關交易的表決。
        that resolution, regardless of the materiality of such interest.

         effective implementation of the remuneration policy, the

      A share option scheme (the “Scheme”) was adopted pursuant to a                                           本公司根據於 2011 年 11 月 17 日的一份股東書面決議

      cancelled under the Scheme since its adoption up to the date of                                          任何購股權。
      this annual report.


      The purpose of the Scheme is to attract skilled and experienced                                          該計劃旨在通過提供獲取本公司股權的機會，吸引有
      personnel, incentivise them to remain with the Group and motivate                                        技能和經驗的人員，激勵他們留任本集團，鼓勵其為
      them to strive for the future development and expansion of the                                           本集團的未來發展及擴展而努力。董事會可酌情根據
      Group by providing them with the opportunity to acquire equity                                           該計劃向其酌情認為對本集團已作出或將作出貢獻的
      interests in the Company. The Board may, at its discretion, grant                                        本公司董事（包括執行董事、非執行董事及獨立非執行
      options pursuant to the Scheme to the directors of the Company                                           董事）、本公司附屬公司的董事、本集團的僱員以及任
      (including executive directors, non-executive directors and                                              何其他人士（包括諮詢人或顧問）授出購股權。
      independent non-executive directors), the directors of the
      Company’s subsidiaries and employees of the Group and any other
      persons (including consultants or advisers) whom the Board
      considers, in its absolute discretion, have contributed or will
      contribute to the Group.

      Permitted Indemnity Provision                                              獲許的彌償條文
      The Company’s Articles provides that every director is entitled to be      本公司細則規定，就其任期內或因執行其職務而可能
      indemnified out of the assets and profits of the Company against all       遭致或發生與此相關之所有損失、損害賠償或責任，
      losses, damages and liabilities which he/she may sustain or incur in or    每位董事有權從本公司資產和溢利中獲得賠償，惟與
      about the execution of duties of his/her office or otherwise in relation   任何董事本身之欺詐或不誠實事宜有關者除外。
      thereto provided that such indemnity shall not extend to any matter in
      respect of fraud or dishonesty which may attach to any director.

      The Company has taken out and maintained directors’ liability              本公司於年內已購買及維持全年的董事責任保險，為
      insurance throughout the year, which provides appropriate cover            董事提供適當的保障。
      for the directors.

      The Board acknowledges the importance of adequate and effective
      risk management and internal control systems which assist them in
      understanding the Group’s risk profile, allocating resources to manage
      key risks and making risk-reward optimised business decisions for
      business planning.

      董事會深明充足有效的風險管理及內部監控系統之重要性，該等系統有助他
      們辨識本集團風險狀況、分配資源以管理主要風險以及衡量風險與回報作出
      最佳的營商決策，以進行業務規劃。

Risk Management Process                                                 風險管理流程
GRMC is responsible for leading the risk management process in          集團風險管理委員會負責按照《企業風險管理手冊》以
an integrated approach in accordance with the Enterprise Risk           綜合方式指導風險管理流程。我們的風險管理流程包
Management Policy Manual. Our risk management process                   括以下步驟：
involves the following steps:


Identification — adopts both top-down and bottom-up approach.           識別 — 同時採納由上而下以及由下而上的方式。


Analysis — assesses the potential consequences and likelihood of        分析 — 評核風險發生的可能性以及潛在後果。
the risks.


Evaluation — evaluates the risk level, risk treatment and priorities.   評價 — 評價風險水平、風險處理方法及優先次序。


Mitigation — recognises the control gaps, assigns risk ownership        應對 — 識別監控差距、委派風險承擔人及確認風險監
and confirms risk control measures.                                     控措施。


Monitoring — conducts evaluations on the design and progress of         監察 — 評價風險監控措施的設計以及實施進度。
implementation of the risk control measures.


Reporting — facilitates risk communications, reviews the                報告 — 促進風險溝通，檢討監控措施後的淨風險水平
residual risk level after control measures and assesses areas for       以及評核需持續改進的範圍。
continuous improvement.




                                                                                        Annual Report 2019 年報
170   Risk Management Report
      風險管理報告




      GRMC adopts both top-down and bottom-up approach to facilitate           集團風險管理委員會同時採納由上而下以及由下而上
      risk identification as it is of paramount importance to understand       的方式以識別風險，因為本集團在有效管理其面對的
      the risk universe that the Group is facing before we can effectively     風險前了解風險全域極為重要。
      manage them.


      Top-down approach                                                        由上而下的方式
      • Members of GRMC and representatives from Executive Office              • 集團風險管理委員會成員與行政部和財務管治中心
         and FGC discuss the risk issues they consider important.                代表討論彼等認為重要的風險事宜。

      • GRMC has various channels for risk identification, for example,        • 集團風險管理委員會由多種途徑識別風險，如從宏
         the principal and emerging risks from macro environment and             觀環境或由企業功能單位上報之策略性目標而產生
         strategic objectives escalated by corporate function units,             的主要及新興風險、透過調查、舉報機制、內部及
         potential control weaknesses indicated through investigation,           外部審核識別之潛在監控弱點，以及由可持續發展
         whistleblowing system, internal and external audit and concerns         委員會傳遞持份者對社會、環境及管治議題的關注
         of our stakeholders on social, environmental and governance             事項。
         issues come through by our Sustainability Committee.

      • Upon approval, the risk management strategy is cascaded to             • 經審批後，風險管理策略會交由有關的業務及功能
         relevant business and functional units to design, implement and         單位進行有關風險監控措施的設計、實施及監察。
         monitor the risk control measures.


      Bottom-up approach                                                       由下而上的方式
      • Heads of business and functional units are required to submit          • 業務及功能單位主管每年最少參加一次風險管理培
         their risk registers for material risks identified along with their     訓暨集思工作坊，他們須提交載有已識別之重大風
         existing control measures at least annually through risk                險和其現行監控措施的風險登記冊。
         management training cum brainstorming workshops.

      • FGC consolidates and validates the risk registers and Executive        • 財務管治中心綜合並驗證風險登記冊，行政部協助
         Office assists the respective senior management to endorse              各高級管理層認同風險水平及優先次序。
         the risk level and priority.

      • GRMC reviews the Group’s consolidated risk register and                • 集 團 風 險 管 理 委 員 會 審 議 集 團 綜 合 風 險 登 記 冊，
         determines the level of attention from GRMC and the                     並判斷集團風險管理委員會及執行委員會需關注的
         Executive Committee.                                                    水平。

Internal Control                                                        內部監控
The structure of our internal control system is based on the            我 們 的 內 部 監 控 系 統 架 構 是 以 Committee of
Committee of Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway                   Sponsoring Organisations of the Treadway
Commission (“COSO”) integrated framework for effective internal         Commission（「COSO」）的 綜 合 框 架 為 基 礎，以 達 致
controls, which comprises 17 principles under five components,          有效的內部監控，該綜合框架由五個部份分 17 項原則
namely, Control Environment, Risk Assessment, Control Activities,       組成，五個部份為控制環境、風險評估、控制活動、
Information and Communication and Monitoring Activities.                訊息與溝通及監控活動。


Our internal control system is integrated into our business             內部監控系統併入我們的業務流程，並有資訊系統及
processes with the support of information system and technology.        技 術 作 支 援。 監 控 目 的、 授 權 及 責 任 以 行 之 有 效 的
Control objectives, authorities and responsibilities, backed by         政策及程序為後盾，而監控活動的表現保存於系統內。
established policies and procedures, and the performance of
control activities are maintained in the system.


Our review of internal controls is closely linked with our risk         我們對內部監控的檢討工作與風險管理框架及流程息
management framework and process. Principal risks and risk              息相關。集團的主要風險和風險管理策略會傳達至業
management strategy of the Group are communicated to heads of           務及功能單位各主管以及內部審核團隊。此舉有助他
business and functional units and internal audit team. This aims at     們進行資源及內控監督工作的分配。在管理層持續評
helping them to allocate their resources and control monitoring         價高風險範圍的監控工作之同時，內部審核團隊將考
efforts. Controls of high risk areas are continually evaluated by       慮是否根據集團重大風險而調整關鍵監控的審核計劃。
management while internal audit team would consider the need of
adjustments on its audit plan on the testing of key controls for
significant risks of the Group.


On an annual basis, members of GRMC, senior management of all           集團風險管理委員會成員、各主要業務及功能單位的
major business and functional units are required to assess the          高級管理人員須每年以自身評價問卷方式，評核內部
effective implementation of internal controls through self-             監控實行的成效，內容涵蓋風險管理及內部監控的成
assessment questionnaire, covering the effectiveness of risk            效，其負責各自業務或職能的法律合規確認。他們亦
management and internal controls as well as confirmation on legal       可藉此承諾全力支持集團就推動有效內部監控工作的
compliance for which they are held accountable. Through this            各個計劃，包括內部監控檢討、內部審核、案件調查
exercise, they also undertake to fully support various Group            及舉報機制。
programmes which promote effective internal control practices,
including internal control review, internal audit, case investigation
and whistleblowing system.

Intensity of market competition                                            激烈的市場競爭
A challenging macroeconomic environment coupled with the dampened          具挑戰性的宏觀環境加上消費意欲減弱，使珠寶市場的
consumer sentiment intensifies the competitive rivalry in the jewellery    競爭加劇。倘我們未能妥善應對這情況及加強競爭優
market. If we fail to navigate the situations well and fortify our         勢，或會對我們的市場份額，甚至銷售及增長造成不利
competitive advantage, it may adversely affect our market share and        影響。
hence hamper our sales and growth.

We control and mitigate the risks by:                                      我們監控和緩和這些風險的方法如下：

• Adopting a multi-brand strategy to enrich our product offerings and      • 採取多品牌策略，豐富產品供應及差異化，以擴大顧
  differentiation that broaden the spectrum of customer segment,              客層面，由較成熟顧客至年輕一代不等。
  ranging from sophisticated customers to young millennials.

• Expanding our business footprint to Southeast Asia and overseas          • 將業務拓展至東南亞及海外市場，以受益於快速發展
  markets to reap benefits from the fast-growing Chinese outbound             的中國出境旅遊市場。
  tourism market.

• Deepening our market penetration in Mainland China and overseas          • 透過加盟模式以及與當地珠寶零售商合作，深化在
  markets through franchise model in county level cities and                  中國內地縣級城鎮及海外市場的滲透率。
  partnership with local jewellery retailers.

      Operational risks                                                              營運風險


       Cybersecurity and data privacy                                                網絡安全及數據私穩
       We pursue emerging technologies to develop our products and services          我們追求新興科技，開發產品及服務和提高營運效率，
       and improve our operational efficiency, but the adoption of technologies      但應用科技的同時亦使我們面臨新的數碼漏洞。主要
       also exposes us to new digital vulnerabilities. Key threats include           威脅包括資訊系統中斷或安全漏洞；因破壞性的惡意
       interruption or security breach to our information system; leakage of         軟件或勒索軟件攻擊而導致顧客個人數據外洩；及科技
       customers’ personal data resulting from destructive malware and               服務供應商未能持續提供安全升級。倘若我們未能應對
       ransomware attack; and technological service vendors fail to provide          科技湧入所帶來的威脅，或會對我們的聲譽、營運及
       ongoing security updates. If we fail to address the threats presented by      財務狀況造成重大不利影響。
       the influx of technology, it may have a material adverse effect on our
       reputation, operations and financial condition.

       We control and mitigate the risks by:                                         我們監控和緩和這些風險的方法如下：

       • Developing and rolling out the cyber incident response plan, which          • 開發及推行網絡事故應對計劃，指導我們以有計劃及
         guides us to address and manage the aftermath of a security breach           協調的方式應對及管理安全漏洞或網絡攻擊帶來的
         or cyberattack in a planned and coordinated approach.                        後果。

       • Conducting regular cyberattack drills, rehearsal and system                 • 定期進行網絡攻擊演習、預演及系統滲透測試，幫助
         penetration tests to help our staff to be prepared and practised in          員工有準備及熟練地應對網絡事故。
         responding to cyber incidents.

       • Performing a maturity assessment on our cybersecurity programme             • 根據美國國家標準與技術研究院 (NIST) 的評估程序及
         based on the assessment procedures and requirements of the                   規定對我們的網絡安全程式進行成熟度評估，有助我
         National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST), which helps           們評估對網絡事故的辨識、保障、偵測、回應及修復
         us to identify areas of weakness and strength in identifying,                的弱點及能力。
         protecting against, detecting, responding to and recovering from
         cyber incidents.

       • Offering tailored cybersecurity and data privacy trainings for (i) senior   • 向以下人士提供定制的網絡安全及數據私隱培訓：
         management to strengthen their ability to manage cyber incidents;            (i) 高級管理層，以加強他們管理網絡事故的能力；及
         and (ii) general staff to reduce the occurrence of security or data          (ii) 一般員工，以減少因員工疏忽而造成的安全或數據
         breaches resulting from employee negligence.                                 漏洞。

       • Assessing the impact of latest data privacy regulations on our              • 評估最新的數據私隱條例（包括歐盟《一般資料保護
         operation, including the GDPR in the EU and the China’s                      規範》及中國《網絡安全法》）對我們營運的影響。
         Cybersecurity Law.

Information technology and data governance                                  資訊科技及數據管治
We are committed to harnessing technological innovations in our             我們致力於業務發展和數碼轉型善用科技創新。倘若我
business development and digital transformation. Our business growth        們建立的資訊科技和有關資源未能配合業務發展及未能
and results of operations may be adversely and materially affected if our   提供可靠的營運或財務數據，可能對我們的業務增長和
IT set up and resources could not match with our business development       經營業績構成重大不利影響。
and generate reliable operational or financial data.

We control and mitigate the risks by:                                       我們監控和緩和這些風險的方法如下：

• Reviewing IT strategy alongside business strategy by the IT               • 資訊科技管治委員會每季審閱資訊科技策略及業務策
  Governance Committee each quarter to ensure IT resources                     略，以確保資訊科技資源分配及風險承受度與業務的
  allocation and risk appetite aligned with business sustainable growth.       可持續增長一致。

• Establishing a dedicated IT business analyst team to facilitate           • 成立專責的資訊科技業務分析團隊，促進資訊科技單
  effective communication between IT and business units to solve               位與業務單位之間的有效溝通，有系統地解決業務問
  business issues systematically.                                              題。

• Fostering common understanding of data ownership, data quality and        • 透過各業務單位的協作及試行計劃，在集團內建立數
  performance metrics across the Group through collaboration and pilot         據擁有權、數據質量及績效指標的共識。
  projects with various business units.

• Designing and implementing data repository to enhance the structure       • 設計及實行數據儲存庫，以改善數據收集、儲存、組
  and process of data capturing, storage, organisation and security for        織及安全的架構及程序作有效分析、分享及報告。
  effective analysis, sharing and reporting.

       Sources of diamonds                                                          鑽石來源
       The irruption of synthetic or unethically sourced diamonds could impact      合成或不合乎道德方式採購的鑽石流入，可影響我們的
       our brand image and operations.                                              品牌形象及營運。

       We control and mitigate the risks by:                                        我們監控和緩和這些風險的方法如下：

       • Maintaining rigorous controls on supplier management, covering the         • 嚴謹監控供應商管理，包括遵行選用和評核供應商的
         policy and procedure for engaging and evaluating our suppliers, to            政策和程序，以確保向可信賴的供應商採購鑽石。
         ensure our diamonds are sourced from trustworthy suppliers.

       • Deploying advanced devices and engaging third party professionals          • 設置嶄新設備及委聘第三方專業人士鑒定鑽石產品的
         to authenticate our diamond products.                                         真偽。

       • Offering trainings for staff to strengthen their knowledge on the market   • 向員工提供培訓，加強他們對合成鑽石的市場趨勢、
         trend, detection techniques and technology of synthetic diamonds.             偵測技巧及技術的了解。

       • Introducing T MARK diamond brand to reinforce our efforts in ensuring      • 推出 T MARK 鑽石品牌，以進一步確保我們的鑽石天
         that our diamonds are natural and authentic and customers can trace           然唯真，來源可靠，且顧客可利用 T MARK 手機應用
         the origin of their diamonds with the use of T MARK mobile app.               程式追蹤鑽石來源。

Basis for Opinion                                                       意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with International Standards       我們已根據《國際審計準則》進行審計。我們在該等準
on Auditing (“ISAs”). Our responsibilities under those standards        則下承擔的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報

of the Consolidated Financial Statements section of our report.

and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion.                     我們的審計意見提供基礎。


Independence                                                            獨立性

Ethics for Professional Accountants (“IESBA Code”), and we have         會計師道德守則》中的其他專業道德責任。
fulfilled our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with the
IESBA Code.

judgment, were of most significance in our audit of the                 合財務報表的審計最為重要的事項。這些事項是在我

matters were addressed in the context of our audit of the               我們不會對這些事項提供單獨的意見。

opinion thereon, and we do not provide a separate opinion on
these matters.

                                                                    解其工作範疇和委聘條款。我們挑選所有珠寶珍藏並觀察獨
立專業珠寶鑒定師參照國際鑽石價格和現時市況進行估值、核實
                                                                    立專業珠寶鑒定師進行的估值程序，並與珠寶估值報告對
真品及根據寶石測試及其專業知識評核珠寶珍藏的價值及狀況。
                                                                    照，測試所記錄的價值是否按成本與可收回金額兩者中的較
                                                                    低者入賬。


                                                                    根據我們進行的程序，我們認為有足夠可得證據支持珠寶珍
                                                                    藏的估值。

     IFRS 15 Revenue from contracts with customers                           國際財務報告準則第 15 號客戶合約收入
     IFRS 15 establishes a comprehensive framework for                       國際財務報告準則第 15 號建立確認客戶合約收
     recognising revenue from contracts with customers. It                   入的全面框架。此準則取代國際會計準則第 11

     construction contracts and IAS 18 “Revenue” which covers                第 18 號「收入」（涵蓋商品及服務合約及有關事
     contracts for goods and services and the related literature.            宜）。應用國際財務報告準則第 15 號後，會計政
     The adoption of IFRS 15 resulted in changes in accounting               策有所改變，亦需對綜合財務報表內確認的收入
     policies and adjustments to the amounts of revenue                      金額作出調整。本集團於截至 2019 年 3 月 31 日
     recognised in the consolidated financial statements. The                止年度綜合財務報表就過渡應用經修改追溯方
     Group adopted a modified retrospective approach for                     式，允許本集團將初次應用國際財務報告準則第
     transition in the consolidated financial statements for the             15 號 的 累計 影 響 確認 為 2018 年 4 月 1 日 期初權
     year ended 31 March 2019, which allows the Group to                     益結餘之調整，而之前期間的比較數字則並無重
     recognise the cumulative effects of initially applying IFRS 15          述。 於 2018 年 4 月 1 日， 本 集 團 就 其 合 約 選 擇
     as an adjustment to the opening balance of equity at 1 April            應用國際財務報告準則第 15 號。
     2018, while prior period comparative figures were not
     restated. The Group chose to apply IFRS 15 for its contracts
     at 1 April 2018.


     Customer loyalty programme                                              客戶忠誠度計劃
     Under the Group’s customer loyalty programme, the customers             根據本集團客戶忠誠度計劃，顧客於若干次數購
     are granted cash coupons or points upon certain purchases,              物後會獲贈現金禮券或積分，此計劃向顧客授出
     which provides a material right to the customers and gives rise         重大的權利，使本公司須承擔獨立的履約責任。
     to a separate performance obligation. Prior to adoption of IFRS         採納國際財務報告準則第 15 號前，交易價格的
     15, portion of the transaction price are allocated to such              部分使用現金禮券或積分的公平值分配至相關客
     customers right using the fair value of cash coupons or points          戶權利，並就已授出但未贖回或未逾期的現金禮
     and recognised as deferred revenue for the cash coupons or              券或積分確認遞延營業額。根據國際財務報告準
     points granted but not yet redeemed or expired. Under IFRS 15,          則第 15 號，交易價格的部分根據相關獨立售價
     portion of the transaction price are allocated to such customers        分配予相關客戶權利。營業額於額外商品或服務
     right based on the relative standalone selling price. Revenue is        轉移至客戶時確認。未行使的權利被視為「未用
     recognised when the additional goods or services are                    量」。本集團將根據客戶行使權利模式的比例確
     transferred to the customers. Unexercised rights are referred to        認預期未用量金額為營業額。
     as breakage. The expected breakage amount should be
     recognised as revenue in proportion to the pattern of rights
     exercised by the customers.

     International Financial Reporting                                            準則（續）
     Standards (“IFRSs”) (Continued)
     New standards and interpretations not yet adopted                            尚未採納的新準則及詮釋
     IFRS 16 Leases                                                               國際財務報告準則第 16 號租賃
     Currently the Group entered into various operating leases as                 如附註 31 披露，目前本集團已訂立若干經營租
     disclosed in note 31, while some leases as the lessor and                    賃，當中均涉及作為出租人及承租人的租賃。國
     others as the lessee. IFRS 16 will primarily affect the Group’s              際財務報告準則第 16 號將主要影響本集團作為
     accounting as a lessee and will result in the recognition of                 承租人的會計，並將導致將絕大部分租約於綜合
     almost all leases on the consolidated statement of financial                 財務狀況表確認。該準則刪除了目前經營租賃及
     position. The standard removes the current distinction                       融資租賃的區別，並要求幾乎所有租賃合約資產
     between operating and financing leases and requires                          （使用租賃項目的權利）及金融負債（支付租金）
     recognition of an asset (the right to use the lease item) and a              的確認。
     financial liability to pay rentals for virtually all lease contracts.


     The application of the new accounting model is expected to                   預期應用新會計模式將導致資產及負債增加，並
     lead to an increase in both assets and liabilities and to impact             影響於租賃期間在綜合損益表確認開支的時間。
     on the timing of the expense recognition in the consolidated                 因此，綜合損益表將受到影響，因為開支總額一
     statement of profit or loss over the period of the lease. As a               般於租約較早年度較高，而於較後年度較低。此
     result, the consolidated statement of profit or loss will be                 外，租金開支將由利息及折舊開支取代。另外，
     affected because the total expenses is typically higher in the               租賃支出亦將不再於現金流量表內呈列為經營現
     earlier years of a lease and lower in later years. Additionally,             金 流 出，但 將 會 列 入 作 為 融 資 現 金 流 出 的 一 部
     rental expenses will be replaced with interest and                           分。新確認的租賃負債的利息支出或會自經營業
     depreciation charges. In addition, leasing expenses will no                  務或融資業務中之現金流呈列。
     longer be presented as operating cash outflows in the
     statement of cash flows, but will be included as part of the
     financing cash outflow. Interest expenses from the newly
     recognised lease liability may be presented in the cash flow
     from operating or from financing activities.


     The Group plans to elect the modified retrospective approach                 本集團計劃就採納國際財務報告準則第 16 號選
     for the adoption of IFRS 16 and will recognise the cumulative                用經修改追溯方式，並將初次應用的累計影響確
     effect of initial application as an adjustment to the opening                認 為 於 2019 年 4 月 1 日 權 益 期 初 結 餘 的 調 整，
     balance of equity at 1 April 2019 and will not restate the                   並 不 會 重 列 比 較 資 料。在 國 際 財 務 報 告 準 則 第
     comparative information. As allowed by IFRS 16, the Group                    16 號允許的情況下，本集團計劃使用可行權宜
     plans to use the practical expedient to grandfather the previous             方法，處理現有安排為租賃或包含租賃的先前評
     assessment of which existing arrangements are, or contain,                   估。因 此，本 集團 僅 將 國 際財 務 報告 準則 第 16
     leases. The Group will therefore apply the new definition of a               號中對租賃的新定義應用於初次應用日期或之後
     lease in IFRS 16 only to contracts that are entered into on or               訂立的合約。本集團預期過渡調整將分別對資產
     after the date of initial application. The Group expects the                 總值及負債總值造成約3%–5%及7%–9%影響。
     transition adjustment would affect the total assets and liability
     by approximately 3%–5% and 7%–9% respectively.

            period, as explained in the accounting policies below.

            consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                 的公平值。

            by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved                本公司：
            when the Company:


            • has power over the investee;                                          • 對投資對象有控制權；

                involvement with the investee; and                                    或享有權力；及


            • has the ability to use its power to affect those returns              • 有能力運用其控制實體活動的權力影響其回
                through its power to direct the activities of the entity.             報。

            liabilities assumed in a business combination are, with                    產以及承擔的負債及或然負債初步按收購日期的

            the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-                        或非控股權益應佔被收購實體可識別資產淨值的

            accumulated impairment losses. Gains and losses on the

            arose. The units or groups of units are identified at the                 最低層次（即經營分部）確認。
            lowest level at which goodwill is monitored for internal
            management purposes, being the operating segments.

     of each element have been transferred to the Group, unless             價（包括任何一次性預付款），以土地租賃權益和
     it is clear that both elements are operating leases in which           樓宇租賃權益相關公平值的比例於初始確認時分
     case the entire property is accounted as an operating lease.           配到租賃土地及樓宇部份。
     Specifically, the entire consideration (including any lump-sum
     upfront payments) are allocated between the leasehold land
     and the building elements in proportion to the relative fair
     values of the leasehold interests in the land elements and
     building element at initial recognition.


     To the extent the allocation of the relevant payments can be           倘能可靠地分配相關款項，入賬為經營租賃的租
     made reliably, interest in leasehold land that is accounted for        賃土地權益將於綜合財務狀況表列賬為「土地使
     as an operating lease is presented as “land use rights” in the         用權」，並按租賃年期以直線法攤銷。
     consolidated statement of financial position and is amortised
     over the lease term on a straight-line basis.

           Classification                                                               分類
           The Group classifies its financial assets in the following                   本集團將其金融資產分類為以下各類：
           measurement categories:


           • those to be measured subsequently at fair value                            • 其後按公平值計入其他全面收益或損益
              (either through other comprehensive income                                  的金融資產，及
              (“OCI”) or through profit or loss); and


           • those to be measured at amortised cost.                                    • 以攤銷成本計量的金融資產。


           The classification depends on the entity’s business                          該分類取決於本集團管理金融資產的業務
           model for managing the financial assets and the                              模式以及該資產的合同現金流量特徵。
           contractual terms of the cash flows.


           For assets measured at fair value, gains and losses will                     對於以公平值計量的金融資產，其利得和
           either be recorded in profit or loss or OCI.                                 損失計入損益或其他全面收益。


           Debt investments are reclassified when and only                              債務投資僅當本集團有關該資產的業務模
           when the Group’s business model for managing those                           式發生變化時，才會進行重新分類。
           assets changes.


           Recognition and derecognition                                                確認及終止確認
           Regular way purchases and sales of financial assets                          金融資產的定期購入及出售，均於交易日
           are recognised on trade-date, the date on which the                          （即本集團承諾購入或出售該資產當日）確
           Group commits to purchase or sell the asset. Financial                       認入賬。當本集團從該等金融資產收取現
           assets are derecognised when the rights to receive                           金流量的權利已到期或已被轉讓，及本集
           cash flows from the financial assets have expired or                         團已將擁有權帶來的絕大部分風險和回報
           have been transferred and the Group has transferred                          轉移，則會終止確認該等金融資產。
           substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership.


           Measurement                                                                  計量
           At initial recognition, the Group measures a financial asset                 於初始確認時，本集團按金融資產的公平
           at its fair value plus, in the case of a financial asset not at              值外加（倘為並非按公平值計入損益的金
           fair value through profit or loss (“FVPL”), transaction                      融資產）直接歸屬於收購該金融資產的交
           costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition of the               易成本計量。按公平值計入損益的金融資
           financial asset. Transaction costs of financial assets                       產交易成本於損益表支銷。
           carried at FVPL are expensed in profit or loss.


           Equity instruments                                                           權益工具

           (losses) in the consolidated statement of profit or loss                     綜合損益表的其他收益╱（虧損）淨額。
           as applicable.

     For the purpose of presentation in the statement of cash                  就呈列現金流量表而言，現金及現金等值項目包
     flows, cash and cash equivalents includes cash on hand,                   括 手 頭 現 金、於 財 務 機 構 的 活 期 存 款、其 他 短
     deposits held at call with financial institutions, other short-           期、高流動投資（原到期日為三個月或以下及可
     term, highly liquid investments with original maturities of               隨時轉換為已知金額的現金且所承受價值變動風
     three months or less that are readily convertible to known                險不大）。
     amounts of cash and which are subject to an insignificant
     risk of changes in value.


     Share capital                                                             股本
     Ordinary shares are classified as equity. Incremental costs               普通股乃分類為權益。直接由發行新股或購股權
     directly attributable to the issue of new shares or options are           引致之新增成本在權益中列為所得款項之減少
     shown in equity as a deduction, net of tax, from the proceeds.            （扣除稅項）。


     Trade and other payables                                                  貿易及其他應付款項
     These amounts represent liabilities for goods and services                該等金額指財政年度結束前就已提供予本集團的
     provided to the Group prior to the end of financial year which            貨品及服務的未償付負債。該等金額為無抵押，
     are unpaid. The amounts are unsecured and are usually paid                通常自確認當日起 7 天至 180 天內支付。貿易應
     between 7 and 180 days of recognition. Trade and other                    付款項呈列為流動負債，除非付款並非於報告期
     payables are presented as current liabilities unless payment is           後 12 個月內到期。其最初按其公平值確認及其
     not due within 12 months after the reporting period. They are             後使用實際利息法按攤銷成本計量。
     recognised initially at their fair value and subsequently measured
     at amortised cost using the effective interest method.


     Government grants                                                         政府補貼
     Government grants are recognised at their fair value where                當有合理保證本集團將遵守政府補貼的附帶條
     there is a reasonable assurance that the Group will comply                件，並且將會收取該筆補貼，則會以公平值確認
     with the conditions attaching to them and that the grants will            政府補貼。
     be received.


     Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on a                   於本集團確認擬作補償補貼的有關成本為開支的
     systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                      期間，有系統地在損益內確認政府補貼。
     recognises as expenses the related costs for which the
     grants are intended to compensate.


     Government grants relating to the purchase of property, plant             有關購買物業、廠房及設備的政府補貼列為非流
     and equipment are included in non-current liabilities as other            動負債中的其他負債，並按直線基準於相關資產
     liabilities and are credited to profit or loss on a straight-line         預計使用年期內計入損益。附註 6 載有本集團有
     basis over the expected lives of the related assets. Note 6               關政府補貼的會計法詳情。
     provides further information on how the Group accounts for
     government grants.


     Government grants that are receivable as compensation for                 作為已產生開支或虧損的補償或作為給予本集團
     expenses or losses already incurred or for the purpose of                 即時財務資助而無日後相關成本的應收政府補
     giving immediate financial support to the Group with no                   貼，於可收取期間在損益確認。
     future related costs are recognised in profit or loss in the
     period in which they become receivable.

            Borrowings                                                                  貸款
            Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of                   借 款 最初 乃 按 公 平值（扣 除 已 產生 的 交 易 成 本）
            transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently                     確認。借款其後按攤銷成本計量，如扣除交易成
            measured at amortised cost. Any difference between the                      本之後的所得款項及贖回金額出現差額，則於借
            proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption                      款期內以實際利率法於損益內確認。在貸款很有
            amount is recognised in profit or loss over the period of the               可能部份或全部提取的情況下，就設立貸款融資
            borrowings using the effective interest method. Fees paid on                支 付 的 費 用 乃 確 認 為 貸 款 交 易 成 本。在 此 情 況
            the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                      下，該費用將遞延至提取貸款發生時。在並無跡
            transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable             象顯示該貸款很有可能部份或全部提取的情況
            that some or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this                下，該費用撥充資本作為流動資金服務的預付款
            case, the fee is deferred until the draw down occurs. To the                項，並於其相關融資期間內予以攤銷。
            extent there is no evidence that it is probable that some or all
            of the facility will be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a
            prepayment for liquidity services and amortised over the
            period of the facility to which it relates.


            Borrowings are removed from the balance sheet when the                      當合約中規定的責任解除、取消或屆滿時，借貸
            obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled or            從資產負債表中剔除。已消除或轉讓給另一方的
            expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a                    金融負債的賬面值與已支付代價（包括已轉讓的
            financial liability that has been extinguished or transferred to            非現金資產或所承擔的負債）之間的差額，在損
            another party and the consideration paid, including any non-                益中確認為融資成本。
            cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed, is recognised
            in profit or loss as finance costs.


            Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the                 借款分類為流動負債，惟本集團擁有無條件權利
            Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement of the                 可將負債之清償延遲至報告期後最少十二個月則
            liability for at least 12 months after the reporting period.                除外。


            Gold loans                                                                  黃金借貸
            Gold loans to be repaid by cash are designated as financial                 將以現金償還的黃金借貸屬於按公平值計入損益
            liabilities at fair value through profit or loss as the gold loans          的金融負債，因為黃金借貸組成包含一個或多個
            form part of a contract containing one or more embedded                     嵌入式衍生工具之合約部份。將以實體黃金償還
            derivatives. Gold loans to be repaid by physical gold are                   的黃金借貸分類為按公平值計入損益的負債。
            classified as liabilities at fair value through profit or loss.


            Gain or losses on gold loans are recognised in the                          黃金借貸的收益或虧損於綜合損益表內確認，惟
            consolidated statement of profit or loss, except for the gains              該等按公平值計入損益的金融負債因本集團自身
            or losses arising from the Group’s own credit risk relating                 信貸風險產生的收益或虧損則計入其他全面收益
            financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss which are        呈列，且不會重新分類至綜合損益表。於綜合損
            presented in other comprehensive income with no                             益表確認的公平值收益或虧損淨額並不包括任何
            subsequent reclassification to the consolidated statement of                扣除此等負債的利息。
            profit or loss. The net fair value gain or loss recognised in the
            consolidated statement of profit or loss does not include any
            interest charged on these liabilities.

     Taxation                                                                稅項
     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently              所得稅開支指即期應繳稅項與遞延稅項的總和。
     payable and deferred tax.


     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the            即期應付稅項乃根據年內的應課稅溢利計算。由
     year. Taxable profit differs from “profit before taxation” as           於應課稅溢利不包括須於其他年度應課稅或可扣
     reported in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and            稅的收入或開支項目，亦不包括毋須課稅或不可
     other comprehensive income because it excludes items of                 扣稅的收入或開支項目，故應課稅溢利與綜合損
     income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other               益及其他全面收益表內列賬的「除稅前溢利」不
     years, and it further excludes income or expense items that             同。本集團的即期稅項負債乃採用於各報告期末
     are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for              已實施或實質上已實施的稅率計算。
     current tax is calculated using tax rates that have been
     enacted or substantively enacted by the end of each
     reporting period.


     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                     遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內資產及負債賬面值與
     between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the           計算應課稅溢利時採用的相應稅基之間的暫時性
     consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax             差異確認。所有應課稅暫時性差異一般均確認為
     base used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax            遞延稅項負債。倘可能出現應課稅溢利可用作抵
     liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary          銷所有可扣減暫時性差異，則一般就所有可扣減
     differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for           暫時性差異確認遞延稅項資產。若因商譽或首次
     all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is            確認一項交易之其他資產與負債而產生並無影響
     probable that taxable profits will be available against which           應課稅溢利或會計溢利的暫時性差異，則不會確
     those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such            認該等資產與負債。
     assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary
     difference arises from goodwill or from the initial recognition
     of other assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects
     neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit.

            future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary                性差異利益時有足夠的應課稅溢利且預期在可見

            are only recognised to the extent that it is probable that there

            the end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent                    減至不可能再有足夠應課稅溢利以收回全部或部
            that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will           份資產為止。
            be available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.

            liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and        產期間的預期適用稅率計算。遞延稅項負債及資
            tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by                 產的計量反映本集團預期於報告期末收回或結算
            the end of the reporting period. The measurement of deferred                 資產及負債賬面值的方式產生的稅務結果。
            tax liabilities and assets reflects the tax consequences that
            would follow from the manner in which the Group expects, at
            the end of the reporting period, to recover or settle the

            except when it relates to items that are recognised in other                 收益或直接於權益確認的項目相關者，則亦分別

            Where current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial

            Revenue recognition                                                    營業額確認
            Revenue is measured at the fair value of the consideration             營業額按本集團一般業務過程中出售商品及提供
            received or receivable for the sales of goods and the provision        服務的已收或應收代價的公平值計量。
            of services in the ordinary course of the Group’s business.

            Revenue is recognised when control over a product or                   營業額於產品或服務的控制權轉移至客戶時確
            service is transferred to the customer, at the amount of               認，金額為本集團預期將有權收取之承諾代價，
            promised consideration to which the Group is expected to be            但不包括代表第三方收取的有關金額。營業額不
            entitled, excluding those amounts collected on behalf of third         包括增值稅或其他銷售稅，且乃經扣減任何貿易
            parties. Revenue excludes value added tax or other sales               折扣。
            taxes and is after deduction of any trade discounts.

            Sales of goods — Retail                                                銷售產品 — 零售
            The Group operates a chain of retail stores selling jewellery          本集團營運銷售珠寶產品及鐘錶的連銷零售店。
            products and watches. Revenue from the sale of goods is                銷售商品的營業額在商品於店內轉移予客戶時
            recognised when the product is transferred to the customer             確認。
            who takes delivery in store.

            Payment of the transaction price is due from retail customers          應收零售顧客的交易付款即時到期，款項通常以
            immediately. The payment is usually settled in cash, by credit         現金、信用卡或電子支付結算或間接通過零售商
            cards, by means of electronic payments or settled indirectly           場結算。
            through retail malls.

            Sales of goods — Customers loyalty programme                           銷售產品 — 客戶忠誠度計劃
            Sales of goods that result in award credits for customers,             根據本集團客戶忠誠度計劃向顧客提供獎勵額度
            under the Group’s customer loyalty programme, are                      之貨品銷售入賬為多元營業額交易，且已收或應
            accounted for as multiple element revenue transactions and             收代價之公平值於供應貨品及所給予獎勵額度之
            the fair value of the consideration received or receivable is          間分配。營業額不會於最初銷售交易時確認 —
            allocated between the goods supplied and the award credits             而是遞延至現金券或積分獲兌換及本集團已履行
            granted. Revenue is not recognised at the time of the initial          有關責任時方會確認為營業額。
            sale transaction — but is deferred and recognised as revenue
            when the cash coupons or points are redeemed and the
            Group’s obligations have been fulfilled.

            Accumulated experience is used to estimate the forfeiture of           本集團使用累計經驗估計棄權的獎勵額度為未用
            award credits as breakage which is recognised as contract              量，並已確認為合約負債。預期未用量金額依照
            liabilities. The expected breakage amount is recognised as             客戶行使權利的模式或客戶不大可能行使權利時
            revenue in proportion to the pattern of rights exercised by            按比例確認為營業額。
            the customers or when the likelihood of the customer
            exercising its rights becomes remote.

33. Capital Risk Management                                          33. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that the group              本集團管理資本旨在確保集團實體可持續經營，
   entities will be able to continue as a going concern while          並透過維持債務與權益的最佳平衡而為股東提供
   maximising the return to shareholders through the                   最大回報。
   optimisation of the debt and equity balance.


   The capital structure of the Group consists of bank                 本集團的資本架構包括銀行貸款、黃金借貸和本
   borrowings, gold loans and equity attributable to                   公司股東應佔股權（包括綜合財務報表所披露之
   shareholders of the Company, comprising share capital,              股本、儲備及保留溢利）。
   reserves and retained profits as disclosed in the consolidated
   financial statements.


   The management of the Group reviews the capital structure           本集團管理層定期檢討資本架構。本集團會考慮
   regularly. The Group considers the cost of capital and the          資本成本及各類資本相關的風險，亦透過派付股
   risks associated with each class of capital, and will balance       息、發行新股、籌集銀行貸款及黃金借貸平衡本
   its overall capital structure through the payment of dividends,     集團整體資本架構。
   new share issues as well as the raising of bank borrowings
   and gold loans.

            credit losses experienced within this period. The historical                     虧損率已作調整以反映影響客戶償付應收款項能
            loss rates are adjusted to reflect current and forward-looking                   力的宏觀經濟因素目前及前瞻性資料。本集團已

            the customers to settle the receivables. The Group has                           及失業率為最相關的因素，因此根據該等因素的
            identified the GDP and the unemployment rate of the                              預期變動調整過往虧損率。
            countries in which it sells its goods and services to be the
            most relevant factors, and accordingly adjusts the historical

            Liquidity risk                                                                                                       流動資金風險
            The management of the Group has built an appropriate                                                                 本集團管理層已為本集團的短期及中期資金以及
            liquidity risk management framework for the management of                                                            流動資金管理規定建立適當的流動資金風險管理
            the Group’s short and medium-term funding and liquidity                                                              框架。本集團透過維持銀行融資以及持續監察預
            management requirements. The Group manages liquidity risk                                                            測及實際現金流量及其金融負債之到期情況，管
            by maintaining banking facilities and by continuously                                                                理流動資金風險。
            monitoring forecasted and actual cash flows and the maturity

         The following table presents the fair value of the                                 下表列示了本集團的金融工具按經常性基

         IFRS 13, Fair value measurement. The level into which                              量的層級分類是經參考下列估值技術中使
         a fair value measurement is classified is determined                               用的輸入資料的可觀察性及重要性後釐
         with reference to the observability and significance of                            定的：
         the inputs used in the valuation technique as follows:

         • Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in                                 • 第一級輸入數據乃實體於計算日於活躍
           active markets for identical assets or liabilities that                            市場可以取得相同的資產或負債之未調
           the entity can access at the measurement date;                                     整報價；

           asset or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                             之輸入數據；及


         • Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset                             • 第三級輸入數據乃並非可根據觀察資產
           or liability.                                                                      或負債所得出之輸入數據。
